5e:["$","h2",null,{"id":"browser-lab-fft-and-pca","className":"heading-linked","style":{"fontFamily":"var(--font-serif)","fontWeight":600,"fontSize":"1.5rem","color":"var(--text-strong)","lineHeight":1.25,"marginBottom":"0.6em","marginTop":"2.5em","paddingBottom":"0.4em","borderBottom":"1px solid var(--border-subtle)","scrollMarginTop":"90px"},"children":["Browser lab: FFT and PCA",["$","$L6a",null,{"id":"browser-lab-fft-and-pca"}]]}]
5f:["$","p",null,{"style":{"fontSize":"1rem","color":"var(--text-body)","lineHeight":1.75,"marginBottom":"1.25em","marginTop":0},"children":"Run an FFT on a synthetic signal, then PCA on synthetic 2D data via the SVD."}]
13a:T514,import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

# Part 1: FFT
t = np.linspace(0, 1, 256, endpoint=False)
signal = np.sin(2 * np.pi * 5 * t) + 0.5 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 20 * t)
spectrum = np.fft.fft(signal)
freqs = np.fft.fftfreq(len(t), d=t[1] - t[0])

# Part 2: PCA via SVD
rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
mean = [0, 0]
cov = [[3, 1.5], [1.5, 1]]
data = rng.multivariate_normal(mean, cov, size=200)
data_centered = data - data.mean(axis=0)
U, S, Vt = np.linalg.svd(data_centered, full_matrices=False)
explained_variance_ratio = (S ** 2) / np.sum(S ** 2)

print("explained variance ratio =", explained_variance_ratio)

fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(11, 4.5))
axes[0].plot(freqs[freqs >= 0], np.abs(spectrum[freqs >= 0]))
axes[0].set_title("FFT magnitude spectrum")
axes[0].set_xlabel("frequency (Hz)")

axes[1].scatter(data_centered[:, 0], data_centered[:, 1], alpha=0.4, s=10)
for i in range(2):
    v = Vt[i] * S[i] / np.sqrt(len(data))
    axes[1].plot([0, v[0]], [0, v[1]], linewidth=2,
                 label=f"PC{i+1} ({explained_variance_ratio[i]*100:.1f}%)")
axes[1].set_aspect("equal")
axes[1].legend()
axes[1].set_title("Principal components")
plt.tight_layout()
plt.show()

print("Notice: the spectrum peaks at 5 Hz and 20 Hz, and PC1 carries most of the explained variance.")
